<script>
// Tab switching — event delegation, survives React re-renders
document.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
  var tab = e.target.closest('#airbnb-api-landing .tab-btn');
  if (!tab) return;

  var root = document.getElementById('airbnb-api-landing');
  var key = tab.getAttribute('data-tab-key');

  root.querySelectorAll('.tab-btn').forEach(function(t) {
    t.classList.remove('active');
    t.setAttribute('aria-selected', 'false');
  });
  tab.classList.add('active');
  tab.setAttribute('aria-selected', 'true');

  root.querySelectorAll('.tab-panel').forEach(function(p) {
    if (p.getAttribute('data-tab-key') === key) {
      p.classList.add('active');
    } else {
      p.classList.remove('active');
    }
  });

  // Resize + reset carousels in newly activated panels
  root.querySelectorAll('.tab-panel[data-tab-key="' + key + '"] .carousel-container').forEach(function(c) {
    if (c._resize) c._resize(0);
  });
});

document.addEventListener('keydown', function(e) {
  var tab = e.target.closest('#airbnb-api-landing .tab-btn');
  if (!tab) return;

  var root = document.getElementById('airbnb-api-landing');
  var tabs = Array.from(root.querySelectorAll('.tab-btn'));
  var idx = tabs.indexOf(tab);
  var next;

  if (e.key === 'ArrowRight' || e.key === 'ArrowDown') {
    next = tabs[(idx + 1) % tabs.length];
  } else if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft' || e.key === 'ArrowUp') {
    next = tabs[(idx - 1 + tabs.length) % tabs.length];
  } else if (e.key === 'Home') {
    next = tabs[0];
  } else if (e.key === 'End') {
    next = tabs[tabs.length - 1];
  }
  if (next) { next.click(); next.focus(); e.preventDefault(); }
});

// Carousel init — sizes cards on load; stores _resize for tab-switch reflow
(function() {
  var root = document.getElementById('airbnb-api-landing');
  if (!root) return;

  root.querySelectorAll('.carousel-container').forEach(function(container) {
    var viewport = container.querySelector('.carousel-viewport');
    var track = container.querySelector('.carousel-track');
    var cards = Array.from(track.children);
    var prevBtn = container.querySelector('.carousel-prev');
    var nextBtn = container.querySelector('.carousel-next');
    var gap = 24;
    var currentIndex = 0;
    var total = cards.length;

    function getVisible() {
      var cw = cards[0].offsetWidth || 450;
      return Math.max(1, Math.floor((viewport.offsetWidth + gap) / (cw + gap)));
    }

    function goTo(idx) {
      var visible = getVisible();
      currentIndex = Math.max(0, Math.min(idx, total - visible));
      var cw = cards[0].offsetWidth;
      track.style.transform = 'translateX(-' + (currentIndex * (cw + gap)) + 'px)';
      prevBtn.disabled = currentIndex === 0;
      nextBtn.disabled = currentIndex >= total - visible;
    }

    function resize(resetIdx) {
      goTo(resetIdx !== undefined ? 0 : currentIndex);
    }

    container._resize = resize;

    resize();
    window.addEventListener('resize', function() { resize(); });
  });
})();

// Carousel button clicks — event delegation so clicks survive ReadMe's React re-renders
document.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
  var btn = e.target.closest('#airbnb-api-landing .carousel-btn');
  if (!btn) return;
  var container = btn.closest('.carousel-container');
  if (!container) return;

  var track = container.querySelector('.carousel-track');
  var viewport = container.querySelector('.carousel-viewport');
  var cards = Array.from(track.children);
  var prevBtn = container.querySelector('.carousel-prev');
  var nextBtn = container.querySelector('.carousel-next');
  var gap = 24;
  var total = cards.length;

  var cw = cards[0].offsetWidth;
  if (!cw) return;

  var visible = Math.max(1, Math.floor((viewport.offsetWidth + gap) / (cw + gap)));

  // Derive currentIndex from the track's current transform
  var match = (track.style.transform || '').match(/translateX\(-([\d.]+)px\)/);
  var currentIndex = match ? Math.round(parseFloat(match[1]) / (cw + gap)) : 0;

  var newIndex = currentIndex + (btn.classList.contains('carousel-next') ? 1 : -1);
  newIndex = Math.max(0, Math.min(newIndex, total - visible));

  track.style.transform = 'translateX(-' + (newIndex * (cw + gap)) + 'px)';
  prevBtn.disabled = newIndex === 0;
  nextBtn.disabled = newIndex >= total - visible;
});

\n  width: 100%; /*adds space on L&R */\n  margin: auto;\n  padding-top: 0.1em; /*separates from 3column div*/\n  padding-bottom: 0.1em; /*separates from lower header*/\n}\n\n/* container to fit content */\n.landing-shortbox-cert {\n  text-align: left;\n  border-radius: 8px;\n  box-shadow: 0;\n  padding: 1em;\n  border: none;\n}\n\n/* heading style */\n.cert-header {\n  color: #FF385C;\n  font-weight: bold;\n}\n\n/* feature button style */\n.btnWrapper  {\n    color: #717171;\n    text-decoration: none !important;\n}\n.btnWrapper button:hover,\n.btnWrapper button:active,\n.btnWrapper button:focus {\n    color: #FF385C;\n    cursor: pointer;\n}\n.btnWrapper button {\n    background-color: white;\n    border: none;\n    display: block;\n    font-size: 1em;\n    padding: 0.5em;\n}\n\n/* feature list style */\nul.feature-list {\n  list-style-type: square;\n    columns: 2;\n  -webkit-columns: 2;\n  -moz-columns: 2;\n}\nul.feature-list li {\n  margin-left: -2em;\n  margin-bottom: 0.5em;\n}\n\n/* test cases formatting  */\n.testCases>div {\n    display: none;\n}\n.testCases>div.active {\n    display: block;\n    clear: right;\n}","stylesheet_hub3":"","javascript":"","javascript_hub2":"// === New: Airbnb Partner API Roadmap - sort/filter ===\n// Placed FIRST and deliberately: this block is plain vanilla JS with no jQuery dependency.\n// The two legacy blocks below use jQuery ($) with no guard for whether jQuery is actually\n// loaded on the current page. If it isn't (e.g. on a ReadMe React-app-rendered doc page,\n// the same category the first legacy block already guards against via useReactApp), `$` is\n// undefined and referencing it throws a ReferenceError. In a synchronous \u003cscript>, an\n// uncaught error aborts the rest of that script — so if this block were placed after the\n// jQuery blocks, a missing-jQuery page would silently skip the roadmap logic entirely, which\n// is exactly what happened before this fix (root cause of \"nothing filters, quarters never\n// populate\": the jQuery blocks below threw before this code ever ran). Keep this block first.\n//\n// Self-scoped: does nothing unless the roadmap page's #roadmap-sort control exists on the\n// current page. Uses a MutationObserver (not a one-time DOMContentLoaded init) because ReadMe\n// pages compile as MDX/JSX with client-side routing: DOMContentLoaded fires once per browser\n// session and never again on in-app navigation, and/or the markup can mount a beat after\n// DOMContentLoaded on a hard load. The observer re-checks on every DOM mutation;\n// initRoadmapSortFilter's own data-bound guard makes repeat calls on an already-initialized\n// element a no-op, so it's safe to leave running indefinitely.\n(function () {\n  function initRoadmapSortFilter() {\n    var sortSelect = document.getElementById('roadmap-sort');\n    var quarterSelect = document.getElementById('roadmap-quarter-filter');\n    var tagSelect = document.getElementById('roadmap-tag-filter');\n    var newOnlyCheck = document.getElementById('filter-new-only');\n    var searchInput = document.getElementById('roadmap-search');\n    if (!sortSelect || !quarterSelect || !tagSelect || !newOnlyCheck || !searchInput) return; // not the roadmap page, do nothing\n    if (sortSelect.getAttribute('data-bound') === 'true') return; // already initialized this element\n    sortSelect.setAttribute('data-bound', 'true');\n\n    var allCards = Array.prototype.slice.call(document.querySelectorAll('.card'));\n\n    // --- Column header count: capture each column's total once, before any filtering ---\n    document.querySelectorAll('.roadmap-col').forEach(function (col) {\n      col.setAttribute('data-total-count', String(col.querySelectorAll('.card').length));\n    });\n\n    // --- \"New\" badge: state-based, not time-based (rewritten 2026-07-08, replacing an earlier\n    // 14-day-window/data-added-at timer). A card is \"new\" if and only if it's in the Upcoming\n    // column AND has no changelog link yet (no \u003ca> inside its \u003ch3>, so a plain-text title).\n    // This deliberately excludes the \"second card\" of a milestone split (e.g. Super Strict:\n    // Auto-Migration Deadline, SSF: Complete) even though those are Upcoming, because they reuse\n    // the same changelog link as their already-announced sibling card, the underlying feature\n    // isn't unannounced, only that specific milestone is still ahead. It stays \"new\" for as long\n    // as the card has no link, not on a timer, once a changelog gets added the badge disappears\n    // on its own, no manual cleanup needed.\n    allCards.forEach(function (card) {\n      var col = card.closest('.roadmap-col');\n      var isUpcomingCard = col && col.getAttribute('data-col') === 'upcoming';\n      var hasLink = !!card.querySelector('h3 a');\n      var isNew = isUpcomingCard && !hasLink;\n      if (isNew) {\n        var badge = document.createElement('span');\n        badge.className = 'new-badge';\n        badge.textContent = 'New';\n        card.appendChild(badge);\n        card.setAttribute('data-is-new', 'true');\n      } else {\n        card.setAttribute('data-is-new', 'false');\n      }\n    });\n\n    // --- Quarter filter: populate options from the cards actually present ---\n    var quarters = [];\n    allCards.forEach(function (card) {\n      var q = card.getAttribute('data-quarter');\n      if (q && quarters.indexOf(q) === -1) quarters.push(q);\n    });\n    quarters.sort();\n    quarters.forEach(function (q) {\n      var opt = document.createElement('option');\n      opt.value = q;\n      opt.textContent = q.replace('-', ' ');\n      quarterSelect.appendChild(opt);\n    });\n\n    function applyToColumn(col) {\n      var cards = Array.prototype.slice.call(col.querySelectorAll('.card'));\n\n      // Filter: search (feature name), tag (single selection), quarter, new-only\n      var searchVal = searchInput.value.trim().toLowerCase();\n      var tagVal = tagSelect.value;\n      var quarterVal = quarterSelect.value;\n      var newOnly = newOnlyCheck.checked;\n\n      cards.forEach(function (card) {\n        var cardTags = (card.getAttribute('data-tags') || '').split(',').map(function (t) { return t.trim(); });\n        var titleEl = card.querySelector('h3');\n        var titleText = titleEl ? titleEl.textContent.toLowerCase() : '';\n        var searchMatch = !searchVal || titleText.indexOf(searchVal) !== -1;\n        var tagsMatch = tagVal === 'all' || cardTags.indexOf(tagVal) !== -1;\n        var quarterMatch = quarterVal === 'all' || card.getAttribute('data-quarter') === quarterVal;\n        var newMatch = !newOnly || card.getAttribute('data-is-new') === 'true';\n        var visible = searchMatch && tagsMatch && quarterMatch && newMatch;\n        card.classList.toggle('js-hidden', !visible);\n      });\n\n      // Update the header count to \"visible/total\" so it reflects the active filters\n      var visibleCount = cards.filter(function (card) { return !card.classList.contains('js-hidden'); }).length;\n      var total = col.getAttribute('data-total-count');\n      var countEl = col.querySelector('.col-count');\n      if (countEl && total) countEl.textContent = visibleCount + '/' + total;\n\n      // Sort\n      var mode = sortSelect.value;\n      var sorted = cards.slice();\n      if (mode === 'default') {\n        sorted.sort(function (a, b) {\n          return parseInt(a.getAttribute('data-order'), 10) - parseInt(b.getAttribute('data-order'), 10);\n        });\n      } else if (mode === 'chrono-desc' || mode === 'chrono-asc') {\n        sorted.sort(function (a, b) {\n          var qa = a.getAttribute('data-quarter');\n          var qb = b.getAttribute('data-quarter');\n          return mode === 'chrono-desc' ? qb.localeCompare(qa) : qa.localeCompare(qb);\n        });\n      } else if (mode === 'breaking-first') {\n        sorted.sort(function (a, b) {\n          var ba = a.getAttribute('data-breaking') === 'true' ? 0 : 1;\n          var bb = b.getAttribute('data-breaking') === 'true' ? 0 : 1;\n          if (ba !== bb) return ba - bb;\n          return parseInt(a.getAttribute('data-order'), 10) - parseInt(b.getAttribute('data-order'), 10);\n        });\n      }\n\n      // Pin \"New\" cards to the top of the Upcoming column, but ONLY in the default view.\n      // Bug found 2026-07-08: this used to run for every sort mode, which meant picking\n      // \"Breaking changes first\" or a chronological sort on Upcoming looked broken, the New\n      // cards got forced to the top regardless, overriding the chosen sort for exactly the\n      // cards a user explicitly asked to reorder. Launched never hits this (nothing there is\n      // ever \"new\"), which is why the symptom only showed up on Upcoming. Once someone picks\n      // an explicit sort mode, respect it fully across the whole column; New-pinning is a\n      // default-view affordance, not a permanent override.\n      if (mode === 'default' && col.getAttribute('data-col') === 'upcoming') {\n        sorted.sort(function (a, b) {\n          var an = a.getAttribute('data-is-new') === 'true' ? 0 : 1;\n          var bn = b.getAttribute('data-is-new') === 'true' ? 0 : 1;\n          return an - bn;\n        });\n      }\n\n      sorted.forEach(function (card) { col.appendChild(card); });\n    }\n\n    function applyAll() {\n      document.querySelectorAll('.roadmap-col').forEach(applyToColumn);\n    }\n\n    // Auto-close the Filters \u003cdetails> panel once a filter/sort control inside it is used,\n    // so it doesn't stay open covering cards after the user has made their choice. \u003c/div>\n\u003c/section>\n\n\u003c/div>\n\n\u003cscript>\n// Tab switching — event delegation, survives React re-renders\ndocument.addEventListener('click', function(e) {\n  var tab = e.target.closest('#airbnb-api-landing .tab-btn');\n  if (!tab) return;\n\n  var root = document.getElementById('airbnb-api-landing');\n  var key = tab.getAttribute('data-tab-key');\n\n  root.querySelectorAll('.tab-btn').forEach(function(t) {\n    t.classList.remove('active');\n    t.setAttribute('aria-selected', 'false');\n  });\n  tab.classList.add('active');\n  tab.setAttribute('aria-selected', 'true');\n\n  root.querySelectorAll('.tab-panel').forEach(function(p) {\n    if (p.getAttribute('data-tab-key') === key) {\n      p.classList.add('active');\n    } else {\n      p.classList.remove('active');\n    }\n  });\n\n  // Resize + reset carousels in newly activated panels\n  root.querySelectorAll('.tab-panel[data-tab-key=\"' + key + '\"] .carousel-container').forEach(function(c) {\n    if (c._resize) c._resize(0);\n  });\n});\n\ndocument.addEventListener('keydown', function(e) {\n  var tab = e.target.closest('#airbnb-api-landing .tab-btn');\n  if (!tab) return;\n\n  var root = document.getElementById('airbnb-api-landing');\n  var tabs = Array.from(root.querySelectorAll('.tab-btn'));\n  var idx = tabs.indexOf(tab);\n  var next;\n\n  if (e.key === 'ArrowRight' || e.key === 'ArrowDown') {\n    next = tabs[(idx + 1) % tabs.length];\n  } else if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft' || e.key === 'ArrowUp') {\n    next = tabs[(idx - 1 + tabs.length) % tabs.length];\n  } else if (e.key === 'Home') {\n    next = tabs[0];\n  } else if (e.key === 'End') {\n    next = tabs[tabs.length - 1];\n  }\n  if (next) { next.click(); next.focus(); e.preventDefault(); }\n});\n\n// Carousel init — sizes cards on load; stores _resize for tab-switch reflow\n(function() {\n  var root = document.getElementById('airbnb-api-landing');\n  if (!root) return;\n\n  root.querySelectorAll('.carousel-container').forEach(function(container) {\n    var viewport = container.querySelector('.carousel-viewport');\n    var track = container.querySelector('.carousel-track');\n    var cards = Array.from(track.children);\n    var prevBtn = container.querySelector('.carousel-prev');\n    var nextBtn = container.querySelector('.carousel-next');\n    var gap = 24;\n    var currentIndex = 0;\n    var total = cards.length;\n\n    function getVisible() {\n      var cw = cards[0].offsetWidth || 450;\n      return Math.max(1, Math.floor((viewport.offsetWidth + gap) / (cw + gap)));\n    }\n\n    function goTo(idx) {\n      var visible = getVisible();\n      currentIndex = Math.max(0, Math.min(idx, total - visible));\n      var cw = cards[0].offsetWidth;\n      track.style.transform = 'translateX(-' + (currentIndex * (cw + gap)) + 'px)';\n      prevBtn.disabled = currentIndex === 0;\n      nextBtn.disabled = currentIndex >= total - visible;\n    }\n\n    function resize(resetIdx) {\n      goTo(resetIdx !== undefined ? 0 : currentIndex);\n    }\n\n    container._resize = resize;\n\n If it isn't (e.g. on a ReadMe React-app-rendered doc page,\n// the same category the first legacy block already guards against via useReactApp), `$` is\n// undefined and referencing it throws a ReferenceError. In a synchronous \u003cscript>, an\n// uncaught error aborts the rest of that script — so if this block were placed after the\n// jQuery blocks, a missing-jQuery page would silently skip the roadmap logic entirely, which\n// is exactly what happened before this fix (root cause of \"nothing filters, quarters never\n// populate\": the jQuery blocks below threw before this code ever ran). Keep this block first.\n//\n// Self-scoped: does nothing unless the roadmap page's #roadmap-sort control exists on the\n// current page. Uses a MutationObserver (not a one-time DOMContentLoaded init) because ReadMe\n// pages compile as MDX/JSX with client-side routing: DOMContentLoaded fires once per browser\n// session and never again on in-app navigation, and/or the markup can mount a beat after\n// DOMContentLoaded on a hard load. The observer re-checks on every DOM mutation;\n// initRoadmapSortFilter's own data-bound guard makes repeat calls on an already-initialized\n// element a no-op, so it's safe to leave running indefinitely.\n(function () {\n  function initRoadmapSortFilter() {\n    var sortSelect = document.getElementById('roadmap-sort');\n    var quarterSelect = document.getElementById('roadmap-quarter-filter');\n    var tagSelect = document.getElementById('roadmap-tag-filter');\n    var newOnlyCheck = document.getElementById('filter-new-only');\n    var searchInput = document.getElementById('roadmap-search');\n    if (!sortSelect || !quarterSelect || !tagSelect || !newOnlyCheck || !searchInput) return; // not the roadmap page, do nothing\n    if (sortSelect.getAttribute('data-bound') === 'true') return; // already initialized this element\n    sortSelect.setAttribute('data-bound', 'true');\n\n    var allCards = Array.prototype.slice.call(document.querySelectorAll('.card'));\n\n    // --- Column header count: capture each column's total once, before any filtering ---\n    document.querySelectorAll('.roadmap-col').forEach(function (col) {\n      col.setAttribute('data-total-count', String(col.querySelectorAll('.card').length));\n    });\n\n    // --- \"New\" badge: state-based, not time-based (rewritten 2026-07-08, replacing an earlier\n    // 14-day-window/data-added-at timer). A card is \"new\" if and only if it's in the Upcoming\n    // column AND has no changelog link yet (no \u003ca> inside its \u003ch3>, so a plain-text title).\n    // This deliberately excludes the \"second card\" of a milestone split (e.g. Super Strict:\n    // Auto-Migration Deadline, SSF: Complete) even though those are Upcoming, because they reuse\n    // the same changelog link as their already-announced sibling card, the underlying feature\n    // isn't unannounced, only that specific milestone is still ahead. It stays \"new\" for as long\n    // as the card has no link, not on a timer, once a changelog gets added the badge disappears\n    // on its own, no manual cleanup needed.\n    allCards.forEach(function (card) {\n      var col = card.closest('.roadmap-col');\n      var isUpcomingCard = col && col.getAttribute('data-col') === 'upcoming';\n      var hasLink = !!card.querySelector('h3 a');\n      var isNew = isUpcomingCard && !hasLink;\n      if (isNew) {\n        var badge = document.createElement('span');\n        badge.className = 'new-badge';\n        badge.textContent = 'New';\n        card.appendChild(badge);\n        card.setAttribute('data-is-new', 'true');\n      } else {\n        card.setAttribute('data-is-new', 'false');\n      }\n    });\n\n    // --- Quarter filter: populate options from the cards actually present ---\n    var quarters = [];\n    allCards.forEach(function (card) {\n      var q = card.getAttribute('data-quarter');\n      if (q && quarters.indexOf(q) === -1) quarters.push(q);\n    });\n    quarters.sort();\n    quarters.forEach(function (q) {\n      var opt = document.createElement('option');\n      opt.value = q;\n      opt.textContent = q.replace('-', ' ');\n      quarterSelect.appendChild(opt);\n    });\n\n    function applyToColumn(col) {\n      var cards = Array.prototype.slice.call(col.querySelectorAll('.card'));\n\n      // Filter: search (feature name), tag (single selection), quarter, new-only\n      var searchVal = searchInput.value.trim().toLowerCase();\n      var tagVal = tagSelect.value;\n      var quarterVal = quarterSelect.value;\n      var newOnly = newOnlyCheck.checked;\n\n      cards.forEach(function (card) {\n        var cardTags = (card.getAttribute('data-tags') || '').split(',').map(function (t) { return t.trim(); });\n        var titleEl = card.querySelector('h3');\n        var titleText = titleEl ? titleEl.textContent.toLowerCase() : '';\n        var searchMatch = !searchVal || titleText.indexOf(searchVal) !== -1;\n        var tagsMatch = tagVal === 'all' || cardTags.indexOf(tagVal) !== -1;\n        var quarterMatch = quarterVal === 'all' || card.getAttribute('data-quarter') === quarterVal;\n        var newMatch = !newOnly || card.getAttribute('data-is-new') === 'true';\n        var visible = searchMatch && tagsMatch && quarterMatch && newMatch;\n        card.classList.toggle('js-hidden', !visible);\n      });\n\n      // Update the header count to \"visible/total\" so it reflects the active filters\n      var visibleCount = cards.filter(function (card) { return !card.classList.contains('js-hidden'); }).length;\n      var total = col.getAttribute('data-total-count');\n      var countEl = col.querySelector('.col-count');\n      if (countEl && total) countEl.textContent = visibleCount + '/' + total;\n\n      // Sort\n      var mode = sortSelect.value;\n      var sorted = cards.slice();\n      if (mode === 'default') {\n        sorted.sort(function (a, b) {\n          return parseInt(a.getAttribute('data-order'), 10) - parseInt(b.getAttribute('data-order'), 10);\n        });\n      } else if (mode === 'chrono-desc' || mode === 'chrono-asc') {\n        sorted.sort(function (a, b) {\n          var qa = a.getAttribute('data-quarter');\n          var qb = b.getAttribute('data-quarter');\n          return mode === 'chrono-desc' ? qb.localeCompare(qa) : qa.localeCompare(qb);\n        });\n      } else if (mode === 'breaking-first') {\n        sorted.sort(function (a, b) {\n          var ba = a.getAttribute('data-breaking') === 'true' ? 0 : 1;\n          var bb = b.getAttribute('data-breaking') === 'true' ? 0 : 1;\n          if (ba !== bb) return ba - bb;\n          return parseInt(a.getAttribute('data-order'), 10) - parseInt(b.getAttribute('data-order'), 10);\n        });\n      }\n\n      // Pin \"New\" cards to the top of the Upcoming column, but ONLY in the default view.\n      // Bug found 2026-07-08: this used to run for every sort mode, which meant picking\n      // \"Breaking changes first\" or a chronological sort on Upcoming looked broken, the New\n      // cards got forced to the top regardless, overriding the chosen sort for exactly the\n      // cards a user explicitly asked to reorder. Launched never hits this (nothing there is\n      // ever \"new\"), which is why the symptom only showed up on Upcoming. Once someone picks\n      // an explicit sort mode, respect it fully across the whole column; New-pinning is a\n      // default-view affordance, not a permanent override.\n      if (mode === 'default' && col.getAttribute('data-col') === 'upcoming') {\n        sorted.sort(function (a, b) {\n          var an = a.getAttribute('data-is-new') === 'true' ? 0 : 1;\n          var bn = b.getAttribute('data-is-new') === 'true' ? 0 : 1;\n          return an - bn;\n        });\n      }\n\n      sorted.forEach(function (card) { col.appendChild(card); });\n    }\n\n    function applyAll() {\n      document.querySelectorAll('.roadmap-col').forEach(applyToColumn);\n    }\n\n    // Auto-close the Filters \u003cdetails> panel once a filter/sort control inside it is used,\n    // so it doesn't stay open covering cards after the user has made their choice. Only the\n    // controls that live inside .filters-panel do this; #roadmap-search sits outside the\n    // panel (always visible) and isn't affected.\n    var filtersDetails = sortSelect.closest('details.filters-details');\n    function applyAllAndClose() {\n      applyAll();\n      if (filtersDetails) filtersDetails.open = false;\n    }\n\n    sortSelect.addEventListener('change', applyAllAndClose);\n    quarterSelect.addEventListener('change', applyAllAndClose);\n    tagSelect.addEventListener('change', applyAllAndClose);\n    newOnlyCheck.addEventListener('change', applyAllAndClose);\n    searchInput.addEventListener('input', applyAll); // 'input' for live filtering as the user types; search stays open, it's outside the panel\n\n    // Reset button: clears search text and every filter/sort control back to its default,\n    // then reapplies and closes the panel. Optional element, not part of the page-detection\n    // guard above, so older cached markup without it degrades gracefully (button just won't\n    // exist, nothing else breaks).\n    var resetBtn = document.getElementById('roadmap-reset-filters');\n    if (resetBtn) {\n      resetBtn.addEventListener('click', function () {\n        searchInput.value = '';\n        sortSelect.value = 'default';\n        quarterSelect.value = 'all';\n        tagSelect.value = 'all';\n        newOnlyCheck.checked = false;\n        applyAllAndClose();\n      });\n    }\n\n    applyAll(); // set the initial \"N/total\" header state before any filter is touched\n  }\n\n  // Try immediately (covers a hard load where the markup is already present) and on\n  // DOMContentLoaded (covers a hard load where it mounts a beat later).\n  if (document.readyState === 'loading') {\n    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', initRoadmapSortFilter);\n  } else {\n    initRoadmapSortFilter();\n  }\n\n  // Then keep watching. Covers: (a) async/hydration mount after DOMContentLoaded on a hard\n  // load, and (b) ReadMe's client-side routing, where DOMContentLoaded fires only once per\n  // session and never again when the user reaches the roadmap page via an in-app link.\n  var roadmapObserver = new MutationObserver(function () {\n    initRoadmapSortFilter();\n  });\n  roadmapObserver.observe(document.documentElement, { childList: true, subtree: true });\n})();\n\n// === Existing: highlight/focus hotspots for guided-tour clicks (legacy hub UI only) ===\n// Guarded: no-ops entirely if jQuery isn't loaded on this page (e.g. React-app-rendered doc\n// pages, including the roadmap page), instead of throwing and aborting the rest of this script.\nif (window.jQuery && !document.documentElement.classList.contains('useReactApp')) {\n  $(document).ready(function(){\n      $(\"#hisearch\").click(function(){\n        $(\"#hub-search > div > input\").focus().select();\n        $(\"#hub-search > div > input\").css({backgroundColor: \"LightGreen\", border: \"2px solid green\" });\n\n      });\n  });\n\n  $(document).ready(function(){\n      $(\"#hiSE\").click(function(){\n       $(\"#content-head > div > div.col-xs-3 > a\").focus().select();\n        $(\"#content-head > div > div.col-xs-3 > a\").css({backgroundColor: \"LightGreen\", border: \"2px solid green\" });\n\n      });\n  });\n  $(document).ready(function(){\n      $(\"#hiAPI\").click(function(){\n        $(\"#header-nav-right > li:nth-child(1) > a\").focus().select();\n \n  width: 100%; /*adds space on L&R */\n  margin: auto;\n  padding-top: 0.1em; /*separates from 3column div*/\n  padding-bottom: 0.1em; /*separates from lower header*/\n}\n\n/* container to fit content */\n.landing-shortbox-cert {\n  text-align: left;\n  border-radius: 8px;\n  box-shadow: 0;\n  padding: 1em;\n  border: none;\n}\n\n/* heading style */\n.cert-header {\n  color: #FF385C;\n  font-weight: bold;\n}\n\n/* feature button style */\n.btnWrapper  {\n    color: #717171;\n    text-decoration: none !important;\n}\n.btnWrapper button:hover,\n.btnWrapper button:active,\n.btnWrapper button:focus {\n    color: #FF385C;\n    cursor: pointer;\n}\n.btnWrapper button {\n    background-color: white;\n    border: none;\n    display: block;\n    font-size: 1em;\n    padding: 0.5em;\n}\n\n/* feature list style */\nul.feature-list {\n  list-style-type: square;\n    columns: 2;\n  -webkit-columns: 2;\n  -moz-columns: 2;\n}\nul.feature-list li {\n  margin-left: -2em;\n  margin-bottom: 0.5em;\n}\n\n/* test cases formatting  */\n.testCases>div {\n    display: none;\n}\n.testCases>div.active {\n    display: block;\n    clear: right;\n}","js":"// === New: Airbnb Partner API Roadmap - sort/filter ===\n// Placed FIRST and deliberately: this block is plain vanilla JS with no jQuery dependency.\n// The two legacy blocks below use jQuery ($) with no guard for whether jQuery is actually\n// loaded on the current page. If it isn't (e.g. on a ReadMe React-app-rendered doc page,\n// the same category the first legacy block already guards against via useReactApp), `$` is\n// undefined and referencing it throws a ReferenceError. In a synchronous \u003cscript>, an\n// uncaught error aborts the rest of that script — so if this block were placed after the\n// jQuery blocks, a missing-jQuery page would silently skip the roadmap logic entirely, which\n// is exactly what happened before this fix (root cause of \"nothing filters, quarters never\n// populate\": the jQuery blocks below threw before this code ever ran). Keep this block first.\n//\n// Self-scoped: does nothing unless the roadmap page's #roadmap-sort control exists on the\n// current page. Uses a MutationObserver (not a one-time DOMContentLoaded init) because ReadMe\n// pages compile as MDX/JSX with client-side routing: DOMContentLoaded fires once per browser\n// session and never again on in-app navigation, and/or the markup can mount a beat after\n// DOMContentLoaded on a hard load. The observer re-checks on every DOM mutation;\n// initRoadmapSortFilter's own data-bound guard makes repeat calls on an already-initialized\n// element a no-op, so it's safe to leave running indefinitely.\n(function () {\n  function initRoadmapSortFilter() {\n    var sortSelect = document.getElementById('roadmap-sort');\n    var quarterSelect = document.getElementById('roadmap-quarter-filter');\n    var tagSelect = document.getElementById('roadmap-tag-filter');\n    var newOnlyCheck = document.getElementById('filter-new-only');\n    var searchInput = document.getElementById('roadmap-search');\n    if (!sortSelect || !quarterSelect || !tagSelect || !newOnlyCheck || !searchInput) return; // not the roadmap page, do nothing\n    if (sortSelect.getAttribute('data-bound') === 'true') return; // already initialized this element\n    sortSelect.setAttribute('data-bound', 'true');\n\n    var allCards = Array.prototype.slice.call(document.querySelectorAll('.card'));\n\n    // --- Column header count: capture each column's total once, before any filtering ---\n    document.querySelectorAll('.roadmap-col').forEach(function (col) {\n      col.setAttribute('data-total-count', String(col.querySelectorAll('.card').length));\n    });\n\n    // --- \"New\" badge: state-based, not time-based (rewritten 2026-07-08, replacing an earlier\n    // 14-day-window/data-added-at timer). A card is \"new\" if and only if it's in the Upcoming\n    // column AND has no changelog link yet (no \u003ca> inside its \u003ch3>, so a plain-text title).\n    // This deliberately excludes the \"second card\" of a milestone split (e.g. Super Strict:\n    // Auto-Migration Deadline, SSF: Complete) even though those are Upcoming, because they reuse\n    // the same changelog link as their already-announced sibling card, the underlying feature\n    // isn't unannounced, only that specific milestone is still ahead. It stays \"new\" for as long\n    // as the card has no link, not on a timer, once a changelog gets added the badge disappears\n    // on its own, no manual cleanup needed.\n    allCards.forEach(function (card) {\n      var col = card.closest('.roadmap-col');\n      var isUpcomingCard = col && col.getAttribute('data-col') === 'upcoming';\n      var hasLink = !!card.querySelector('h3 a');\n      var isNew = isUpcomingCard && !hasLink;\n      if (isNew) {\n        var badge = document.createElement('span');\n        badge.className = 'new-badge';\n        badge.textContent = 'New';\n        card.appendChild(badge);\n        card.setAttribute('data-is-new', 'true');\n      } else {\n        card.setAttribute('data-is-new', 'false');\n      }\n    });\n\n    // --- Quarter filter: populate options from the cards actually present ---\n    var quarters = [];\n    allCards.forEach(function (card) {\n      var q = card.getAttribute('data-quarter');\n      if (q && quarters.indexOf(q) === -1) quarters.push(q);\n    });\n    quarters.sort();\n    quarters.forEach(function (q) {\n      var opt = document.createElement('option');\n      opt.value = q;\n      opt.textContent = q.replace('-', ' ');\n      quarterSelect.appendChild(opt);\n    });\n\n    function applyToColumn(col) {\n      var cards = Array.prototype.slice.call(col.querySelectorAll('.card'));\n\n      // Filter: search (feature name), tag (single selection), quarter, new-only\n      var searchVal = searchInput.value.trim().toLowerCase();\n      var tagVal = tagSelect.value;\n      var quarterVal = quarterSelect.value;\n      var newOnly = newOnlyCheck.checked;\n\n      cards.forEach(function (card) {\n        var cardTags = (card.getAttribute('data-tags') || '').split(',').map(function (t) { return t.trim(); });\n        var titleEl = card.querySelector('h3');\n        var titleText = titleEl ? titleEl.textContent.toLowerCase() : '';\n        var searchMatch = !searchVal || titleText.indexOf(searchVal) !== -1;\n        var tagsMatch = tagVal === 'all' || cardTags.indexOf(tagVal) !== -1;\n        var quarterMatch = quarterVal === 'all' || card.getAttribute('data-quarter') === quarterVal;\n        var newMatch = !newOnly || card.getAttribute('data-is-new') === 'true';\n        var visible = searchMatch && tagsMatch && quarterMatch && newMatch;\n        card.classList.toggle('js-hidden', !visible);\n      });\n\n      // Update the header count to \"visible/total\" so it reflects the active filters\n      var visibleCount = cards.filter(function (card) { return !card.classList.contains('js-hidden'); }).length;\n      var total = col.getAttribute('data-total-count');\n      var countEl = col.querySelector('.col-count');\n      if (countEl && total) countEl.textContent = visibleCount + '/' + total;\n\n      // Sort\n      var mode = sortSelect.value;\n      var sorted = cards.slice();\n      if (mode === 'default') {\n        sorted.sort(function (a, b) {\n          return parseInt(a.getAttribute('data-order'), 10) - parseInt(b.getAttribute('data-order'), 10);\n        });\n      } else if (mode === 'chrono-desc' || mode === 'chrono-asc') {\n        sorted.sort(function (a, b) {\n          var qa = a.getAttribute('data-quarter');\n          var qb = b.getAttribute('data-quarter');\n          return mode === 'chrono-desc' ? qb.localeCompare(qa) : qa.localeCompare(qb);\n        });\n      } else if (mode === 'breaking-first') {\n        sorted.sort(function (a, b) {\n          var ba = a.getAttribute('data-breaking') === 'true' ? 0 : 1;\n          var bb = b.getAttribute('data-breaking') === 'true' ? 0 : 1;\n          if (ba !== bb) return ba - bb;\n          return parseInt(a.getAttribute('data-order'), 10) - parseInt(b.getAttribute('data-order'), 10);\n        });\n      }\n\n      // Pin \"New\" cards to the top of the Upcoming column, but ONLY in the default view.\n      // Bug found 2026-07-08: this used to run for every sort mode, which meant picking\n      // \"Breaking changes first\" or a chronological sort on Upcoming looked broken, the New\n      // cards got forced to the top regardless, overriding the chosen sort for exactly the\n      // cards a user explicitly asked to reorder. Launched never hits this (nothing there is\n      // ever \"new\"), which is why the symptom only showed up on Upcoming. Once someone picks\n      // an explicit sort mode, respect it fully across the whole column; New-pinning is a\n      // default-view affordance, not a permanent override.\n      if (mode === 'default' && col.getAttribute('data-col') === 'upcoming') {\n        sorted.sort(function (a, b) {\n          var an = a.getAttribute('data-is-new') === 'true' ? 0 : 1;\n          var bn = b.getAttribute('data-is-new') === 'true' ? 0 : 1;\n          return an - bn;\n        });\n      }\n\n      sorted.forEach(function (card) { col.appendChild(card); });\n    }\n\n    function applyAll() {\n      document.querySelectorAll('.roadmap-col').forEach(applyToColumn);\n    }\n\n    // Auto-close the Filters \u003cdetails> panel once a filter/sort control inside it is used,\n    // so it doesn't stay open covering cards after the user has made their choice. Only the\n    // controls that live inside .filters-panel do this; #roadmap-search sits outside the\n    // panel (always visible) and isn't affected.\n    var filtersDetails = sortSelect.closest('details.filters-details');\n    function applyAllAndClose() {\n      applyAll();\n      if (filtersDetails) filtersDetails.open = false;\n    }\n\n    sortSelect.addEventListener('change', applyAllAndClose);\n    quarterSelect.addEventListener('change', applyAllAndClose);\n    tagSelect.addEventListener('change', applyAllAndClose);\n    newOnlyCheck.addEventListener('change', applyAllAndClose);\n    searchInput.addEventListener('input', applyAll); // 'input' for live filtering as the user types; search stays open, it's outside the panel\n\n    // Reset button: clears search text and every filter/sort control back to its default,\n    // then reapplies and closes the panel. Optional element, not part of the page-detection\n    // guard above, so older cached markup without it degrades gracefully (button just won't\n    // exist, nothing else breaks).\n    var resetBtn = document.getElementById('roadmap-reset-filters');\n    if (resetBtn) {\n      resetBtn.addEventListener('click', function () {\n        searchInput.value = '';\n        sortSelect.value = 'default';\n        quarterSelect.value = 'all';\n        tagSelect.value = 'all';\n        newOnlyCheck.checked = false;\n        applyAllAndClose();\n      });\n    }\n\n    applyAll(); // set the initial \"N/total\" header state before any filter is touched\n  }\n\n  // Try immediately (covers a hard load where the markup is already present) and on\n  // DOMContentLoaded (covers a hard load where it mounts a beat later).\n  if (document.readyState === 'loading') {\n    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', initRoadmapSortFilter);\n  } else {\n    initRoadmapSortFilter();\n  }\n\n  // Then keep watching. Covers: (a) async/hydration mount after DOMContentLoaded on a hard\n  // load, and (b) ReadMe's client-side routing, where DOMContentLoaded fires only once per\n  // session and never again when the user reaches the roadmap page via an in-app link.\n  var roadmapObserver = new MutationObserver(function () {\n    initRoadmapSortFilter();\n  });\n  roadmapObserver.observe(document.documentElement, { childList: true, subtree: true });\n})();\n\n// === Existing: highlight/focus hotspots for guided-tour clicks (legacy hub UI only) ===\n// Guarded: no-ops entirely if jQuery isn't loaded on this page (e.g. React-app-rendered doc\n// pages, including the roadmap page), instead of throwing and aborting the rest of this script.\nif (window.jQuery && !document.documentElement.classList.contains('useReactApp')) {\n  $(document).ready(function(){\n      $(\"#hisearch\").click(function(){\n        $(\"#hub-search > div > input\").focus().select();\n        $(\"#hub-search > div > input\").css({backgroundColor: \"LightGreen\", border: \"2px solid green\" });\n\n      });\n  });\n\n  $(document).ready(function(){\n      $(\"#hiSE\").click(function(){\n       $(\"#content-head > div > div.col-xs-3 > a\").focus().select();\n        $(\"#content-head > div > div.col-xs-3 > a\").css({backgroundColor: \"LightGreen\", border: \"2px solid green\" });\n\n      });\n  });\n  $(document).ready(function(){\n      $(\"#hiAPI\").click(function(){\n        $(\"#header-nav-right > li:nth-child(1) > a\").focus().select();\n        $(\"#header-nav-right > li:nth-child(1) > a\").css({backgroundColor: \"LightGreen\", border: \"2px solid green\" });\n\n      });\n  });\n\n  $(document).ready(function(){\n      $(\"#hiPP\").click(function(){\n        $(\"#header-nav-right > li:nth-child(6) > a\").focus().select();\n        $(\"#header-nav-right > li:nth-child(6) > a\").css({backgroundColor: \"LightGreen\", $(document).ready(function(){\n      $(\"#hiThumbs\").click(function(){\n        $(\"#helpful\").focus().select();\n        $(\"#helpful\").css({backgroundColor: \"LightGreen\", border: \"2px solid green\" });\n        location.href = \"#helpful\";\n      });\n  });\n\n  $(document).ready(function(){\n      $(\"#hifooter\").click(function(){\n        $(\"body > div.footerbox > document.body.scrollHeight, behavior: \"smooth\" });\n      });\n  });\n}\n\n// === Existing: certification test cases - click feature to show relevant cases ===\n// Guarded: no-ops entirely if jQuery <script async data-chunk="routes-SuperHub" src="https://cdn.readme.io/public/hub/web/routes-SuperHub.237e1c2894de538072cd.js"></script>
<script async data-chunk="Containers-EndUserContainer" src="https://cdn.readme.io/public/hub/web/Containers-EndUserContainer.0271ffe15b87850fb883.js"></script>

<script async data-chunk="Header" src="https://cdn.readme.io/public/hub/web/5386.d7d0a0b2c8326ff90b3f.js"></script>
<script async data-chunk="Header" src="https://cdn.readme.io/public/hub/web/3105.7e2fcacb1a0de0419031.js"></script>
<script async data-chunk="Header" src="https://cdn.readme.io/public/hub/web/Header.b461005fbc8170eeac60.js"></script>

<script async data-chunk="Home" src="https://cdn.readme.io/public/hub/web/Home.ef24f4c4437e82c5e96c.js"></script>
<script async data-chunk="Footer" src="https://cdn.readme.io/public/hub/web/Footer.897b17385b4f10637073.js"></script></div><script id="customJS">// === New: Airbnb Partner API Roadmap - sort/filter ===
// Placed FIRST and deliberately: this block is plain vanilla JS with no jQuery dependency.
// The two legacy blocks below use jQuery ($) with no guard for whether jQuery is actually
// loaded on the current page. If it isn't (e.g. on a ReadMe React-app-rendered doc page,
// the same category the first legacy block already guards against via useReactApp), `$` is
// undefined and referencing it throws a ReferenceError. In a synchronous <script>, an
// uncaught error aborts the rest of that script — so if this block were placed after the
// jQuery blocks, a missing-jQuery page would silently skip the roadmap logic entirely, which
// is exactly what happened before this fix (root cause of "nothing filters, quarters never
// populate": the jQuery blocks below threw before this code ever ran). Keep this block first.
//
// Self-scoped: does nothing unless the roadmap page's #roadmap-sort control exists on the
// current page. Uses a MutationObserver (not a one-time DOMContentLoaded init) because ReadMe
// pages compile as MDX/JSX with client-side routing: DOMContentLoaded fires once per browser
// session and never again on in-app navigation, and/or the markup can mount a beat after
// DOMContentLoaded on a hard load. The observer re-checks on every DOM mutation;
// initRoadmapSortFilter's own data-bound guard makes repeat calls on an already-initialized
// element a no-op, so it's safe to leave running indefinitely.
(function () {
  function initRoadmapSortFilter() {
    var sortSelect = document.getElementById('roadmap-sort');
    var quarterSelect = document.getElementById('roadmap-quarter-filter');
    var tagSelect = document.getElementById('roadmap-tag-filter');
    var newOnlyCheck = document.getElementById('filter-new-only');
    var searchInput = document.getElementById('roadmap-search');
    if (!sortSelect || !quarterSelect || !tagSelect || !newOnlyCheck || !searchInput) return; // not the roadmap page, do nothing
    if (sortSelect.getAttribute('data-bound') === 'true') return; // already initialized this element
    sortSelect.setAttribute('data-bound', 'true');

    var allCards = Array.prototype.slice.call(document.querySelectorAll('.card'));

    // --- Column header count: capture each column's total once, before any filtering ---
    document.querySelectorAll('.roadmap-col').forEach(function (col) {
      col.setAttribute('data-total-count', String(col.querySelectorAll('.card').length));
    });

    // --- "New" badge: state-based, not time-based (rewritten 2026-07-08, replacing an earlier
    // 14-day-window/data-added-at timer). A card is "new" if and only if it's in the Upcoming
    // column AND has no changelog link yet (no <a> inside its <h3>, so a plain-text title).
    // This deliberately excludes the "second card" of a milestone split (e.g. Super Strict:
    // Auto-Migration Deadline, SSF: Complete) even though those are Upcoming, because they reuse
    // the same changelog link as their already-announced sibling card, the underlying feature
    // isn't unannounced, only that specific milestone is still ahead. It stays "new" for as long
    // as the card has no link, not on a timer, once a changelog gets added the badge disappears
    // on its own, no manual cleanup needed.
    allCards.forEach(function (card) {
      var col = card.closest('.roadmap-col');
      var isUpcomingCard = col && col.getAttribute('data-col') === 'upcoming';
      var hasLink = !!card.querySelector('h3 a');
      var isNew = isUpcomingCard && !hasLink;
      if (isNew) {
        var badge = document.createElement('span');
        badge.className = 'new-badge';
        badge.textContent = 'New';
        card.appendChild(badge);
        card.setAttribute('data-is-new', 'true');
      } else {
        card.setAttribute('data-is-new', 'false');
      }
    });

    // --- Quarter filter: populate options from the cards actually present ---
    var quarters = [];
    allCards.forEach(function (card) {
      var q = card.getAttribute('data-quarter');
      if (q && quarters.indexOf(q) === -1) quarters.push(q);
    });
    quarters.sort();
    quarters.forEach(function (q) {
      var opt = document.createElement('option');
      opt.value = q;
      opt.textContent = q.replace('-', ' ');
      quarterSelect.appendChild(opt);
    });

    function applyToColumn(col) {
      var cards = Array.prototype.slice.call(col.querySelectorAll('.card'));

      // Filter: search (feature name), tag (single selection), quarter, new-only
      var searchVal = searchInput.value.trim().toLowerCase();
      var tagVal = tagSelect.value;
      var quarterVal = quarterSelect.value;
      var newOnly = newOnlyCheck.checked;

      cards.forEach(function (card) {
        var cardTags = (card.getAttribute('data-tags') || '').split(',').map(function (t) { return t.trim(); });
        var titleEl = card.querySelector('h3');
        var titleText = titleEl ? titleEl.textContent.toLowerCase() : '';
        var searchMatch = !searchVal || titleText.indexOf(searchVal) !== -1;
        var tagsMatch = tagVal === 'all' || cardTags.indexOf(tagVal) !== -1;
        var quarterMatch = quarterVal === 'all' || card.getAttribute('data-quarter') === quarterVal;
        var newMatch = !newOnly || card.getAttribute('data-is-new') === 'true';
        var visible = searchMatch && tagsMatch && quarterMatch && newMatch;
        card.classList.toggle('js-hidden', !visible);
      });

      // Update the header count to "visible/total" so it reflects the active filters
      var visibleCount = cards.filter(function (card) { return !card.classList.contains('js-hidden'); }).length;
      var total = col.getAttribute('data-total-count');
      var countEl = col.querySelector('.col-count');
      if (countEl && total) countEl.textContent = visibleCount + '/' + total;

      // Sort
      var mode = sortSelect.value;
      var sorted = cards.slice();
      if (mode === 'default') {
        sorted.sort(function (a, b) {
          return parseInt(a.getAttribute('data-order'), 10) - parseInt(b.getAttribute('data-order'), 10);
        });
      } else if (mode === 'chrono-desc' || mode === 'chrono-asc') {
        sorted.sort(function (a, b) {
          var qa = a.getAttribute('data-quarter');
          var qb = b.getAttribute('data-quarter');
          return mode === 'chrono-desc' ? qb.localeCompare(qa) : qa.localeCompare(qb);
        });
      } else if (mode === 'breaking-first') {
        sorted.sort(function (a, b) {
          var ba = a.getAttribute('data-breaking') === 'true' ? 0 : 1;
          var bb = b.getAttribute('data-breaking') === 'true' ? 0 : 1;
          if (ba !== bb) return ba - bb;
          return parseInt(a.getAttribute('data-order'), 10) - parseInt(b.getAttribute('data-order'), 10);
        });
      }

      // Pin "New" cards to the top of the Upcoming column, but ONLY in the default view.
      // Bug found 2026-07-08: this used to run for every sort mode, which meant picking
      // "Breaking changes first" or a chronological sort on Upcoming looked broken, the New
      // cards got forced to the top regardless, overriding the chosen sort for exactly the
      // cards a user explicitly asked to reorder. Launched never hits this (nothing there is
      // ever "new"), which is why the symptom only showed up on Upcoming. Once someone picks
      // an explicit sort mode, respect it fully across the whole column; New-pinning is a
      // default-view affordance, not a permanent override.
      if (mode === 'default' && col.getAttribute('data-col') === 'upcoming') {
        sorted.sort(function (a, b) {
          var an = a.getAttribute('data-is-new') === 'true' ? 0 : 1;
          var bn = b.getAttribute('data-is-new') === 'true' ? 0 : 1;
          return an - bn;
        });
      }

      sorted.forEach(function (card) { col.appendChild(card); });
    }

    function applyAll() {
      document.querySelectorAll('.roadmap-col').forEach(applyToColumn);
    }

    // Auto-close the Filters <details> panel once a filter/sort control inside it is used,
    // so it doesn't stay open covering cards after the user has made their choice. Only the
    // controls that live inside .filters-panel do this; #roadmap-search sits outside the
    // panel (always visible) and isn't affected.
    var filtersDetails = sortSelect.closest('details.filters-details');
    function applyAllAndClose() {
      applyAll();
      if (filtersDetails) filtersDetails.open = false;
    }

    sortSelect.addEventListener('change', applyAllAndClose);
    quarterSelect.addEventListener('change', applyAllAndClose);
    tagSelect.addEventListener('change', applyAllAndClose);
    newOnlyCheck.addEventListener('change', applyAllAndClose);
    searchInput.addEventListener('input', applyAll); // 'input' for live filtering as the user types; search stays open, it's outside the panel

    // Reset button: clears search text and every filter/sort control back to its default,
    // then reapplies and closes the panel. Optional element, not part of the page-detection
    // guard above, so older cached markup without it degrades gracefully (button just won't
    // exist, nothing else breaks).
    var resetBtn = document.getElementById('roadmap-reset-filters');
    if (resetBtn) {
      resetBtn.addEventListener('click', function () {
        searchInput.value = '';
        sortSelect.value = 'default';
        quarterSelect.value = 'all';
        tagSelect.value = 'all';
        newOnlyCheck.checked = false;
        applyAllAndClose();
      });
    }

    applyAll(); // set the initial "N/total" header state before any filter is touched
  }

  // Try immediately (covers a hard load where the markup is already present) and on
  // DOMContentLoaded (covers a hard load where it mounts a beat later).
  if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', initRoadmapSortFilter);
  } else {
    initRoadmapSortFilter();
  }

  // Then keep watching. Covers: (a) async/hydration mount after DOMContentLoaded on a hard
  // load, and (b) ReadMe's client-side routing, where DOMContentLoaded fires only once per
  // session and never again when the user reaches the roadmap page via an in-app link.
  var roadmapObserver = new MutationObserver(function () {
    initRoadmapSortFilter();
  });
  roadmapObserver.observe(document.documentElement, { childList: true, subtree: true });
})();

// === Existing: highlight/focus hotspots for guided-tour clicks (legacy hub UI only) ===
// Guarded: no-ops entirely if jQuery isn't loaded on this page (e.g. React-app-rendered doc
// pages, including the roadmap page), instead of throwing and aborting the rest of this script.
if (window.jQuery && !document.documentElement.classList.contains('useReactApp')) {
  $(document).ready(function(){
      $("#hisearch").click(function(){
        $("#hub-search > div > input").focus().select();
        $("#hub-search > div > input").css({backgroundColor: "LightGreen", border: "2px solid green" });

      });
  });

  $(document).ready(function(){
      $("#hiSE").click(function(){
       $("#content-head > div > div.col-xs-3 > a").focus().select();
        $("#content-head > div > div.col-xs-3 > a").css({backgroundColor: "LightGreen", border: "2px solid green" });

      });
  });
  $(document).ready(function(){
      $("#hiAPI").click(function(){
        $("#header-nav-right > li:nth-child(1) > a").focus().select();
        $("#header-nav-right > li:nth-child(1) > a").css({backgroundColor: "LightGreen", border: "2px solid green" });

      });
  });

  $(document).ready(function(){
      $("#hiPP").click(function(){
        $("#header-nav-right > li:nth-child(6) > a").focus().select();
        $("#header-nav-right > li:nth-child(6) > a").css({backgroundColor: "LightGreen", border: "2px solid green" });

      });
  });

  $(document).ready(function(){
      $("#hiTOC").click(function(){
        $("#hub-sidebar-content > div:nth-child(1)").focus().select();
        $("#hub-sidebar-content > div:nth-child(1)").css({backgroundColor: "LightGreen", border: "2px solid green" });

      });
  });

  $(document).ready(function(){
      $("#hiThumbs").click(function(){
        $("#helpful").focus().select();
        $("#helpful").css({backgroundColor: "LightGreen", border: "2px solid green" });
        location.href = "#helpful";
      });
  });

  $(document).ready(function(){
      $("#hifooter").click(function(){
        $("body > div.footerbox > p").focus().select();
        $("body > div.footerbox > p").css({backgroundColor: "LightGreen", border: "2px solid green" });
        window.scrollTo({ left: 0, top: document.body.scrollHeight, behavior: "smooth" });
      });
  });
}

          $('.btnWrapper button').on('click', function(){

</script><script id="hub-me" type="application/json" data-json="{&quot;loggedIn&quot;:false,&quot;search&quot;:{&quot;app&quot;:&quot;&quot;,&quot;token&quot;:&quot;&quot;,&quot;filters&quot;:&quot;&quot;,&quot;metaData&quot;:[]}}"></script></body></html>
